        val memBefore = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val hosts = listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8", "9.9.9.9", "www.google.com", "www.aparat.com")
        val isIp = Regex("^\\d{1,3}(\\.\\d{1,3}){3}$")
        val res = hosts.map { h ->
            val icmp = try { Diagnostics.pingOnce(h) != null } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            val tcp = try { Diagnostics.tcpTime(h, 443).ok } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            val dns: Boolean? = if (isIp.matches(h)) null else try { Diagnostics.dnsTime(h).ok } catch (e: Exception) { false }
            HostCheck(h, icmp, tcp, dns, icmp || tcp || (dns == true))
        }
        val okCount = res.count { it.ok }
        val verdict = when {
            okCount == res.size -> "همه ۵ مقصد مستقل سالم‌اند (حداقل یک روش از ICMP/TCP/DNS برای هر کدام جواب داد) — اینترنت و مسیر سالم است."
            okCount == 0 -> "هیچ‌کدام از ۵ مقصد با هیچ‌کدام از سه روش جواب ندادند — اختلال از اینترنت/اتصال خودت است، نه یک مقصد خاص."
            res.last().ok && okCount < 3 -> "مقصد داخلی (آپارات) سالم ولی مقصدهای بین‌المللی خراب‌اند — اختلال بین‌الملل، نه کل اینترنت."
            else -> "بعضی مقصدها سالم، بعضی خراب‌اند ($okCount/${res.size}) — اختلال از مسیر یا مقصد خاص است، نه کل اینترنت."
        }
        val memAfter = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val dur = System.currentTimeMillis() - start
        val perf = "زمان اجرا: ${dur}ms (ICMP تک‌پینگ + TCP + DNS واقعی؛ برای IPها DNS = N/A) • RAM: $memBefore → $memAfter MB • باتری: در این تست کوتاه قابل اندازه‌گیری نیست."
        return MultiHost(res, verdict, dur, perf)
    }

    // DNS Benchmark v1.7: 3 rounds per resolver, Median + P95 + success rate. Cache note: round 1 is cold,
    // rounds 2-3 may be faster due to DNS cache — both are shown, median is reported.
    data class DnsBenchRound(val name: String, val medianMs: Long, val p95Ms: Long, val successPct: Int, val rounds: List<Long>, val cacheNote: String)
    // rounds: configurable, default 20 (minimum recommended for P95). If n < 20, P95 gets a statistical warning.
    fun dnsBenchmarkRounds(rounds: Int = 20): List<DnsBenchRound> {
        val out = mutableListOf<DnsBenchRound>()
        val targets = listOf("Cloudflare" to "1.1.1.1", "Google" to "8.8.8.8", "Quad9" to "9.9.9.9")
        for ((name, ip) in targets) {
            val times = mutableListOf<Long>(); var ok = 0
            repeat(rounds) { val t = Diagnostics.udpDns(ip); if (t.ok) { ok++; times.add(t.ms) } }
            val sorted = times.sorted()
            val median = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[sorted.size / 2]
            val p95 = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[(kotlin.math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.size).toInt().coerceIn(1, sorted.size)) - 1]
            val warn = if (times.size < 20) " ⚠️ هشدار آماری: نمونه کمتر از ۲۰ است، P95 اعتبار پایینی دارد." else ""
            out.add(DnsBenchRound(name, median, p95, if (rounds == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / rounds, times, "دور ۱ سرد (بدون Cache)، دورهای بعد ممکن است به‌خاطر DNS Cache سریع‌تر باشند؛ Median از همه دورها حساب می‌شود.$warn"))
        }
        // DoH rounds
        for ((name, url) in listOf("Cloudflare DoH" to "https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query", "Google DoH" to "https://dns.google/resolve")) {
            val times = mutableListOf<Long>(); var ok = 0
            repeat(rounds) { val s = System.currentTimeMillis(); val ips = Diagnostics.dohIps(url, "google.com"); if (ips.isNotEmpty()) { ok++; times.add(System.currentTimeMillis() - s) } }
            val sorted = times.sorted()
            val median = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[sorted.size / 2]
            val p95 = if (sorted.isEmpty()) 0 else sorted[(kotlin.math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.size).toInt().coerceIn(1, sorted.size)) - 1]
            val warn = if (times.size < 20) " ⚠️ هشدار آماری: نمونه کمتر از ۲۰ است، P95 اعتبار پایینی دارد." else ""
            out.add(DnsBenchRound(name, median, p95, if (rounds == 0) 0 else ok * 100 / rounds, times, "DoH روی HTTPS است؛ Cache مرورگر/سیستم روی آن تأثیر کمتری دارد.$warn"))
        }
        return out
    }

    // Disruption classifier: only with sufficient evidence; otherwise Probable / Unknown.
    data class Classification(val type: String, val confidence: Int, val evidence: String)
    fun classifyDisruption(results: List<ProbeResult>, filtering: String, gatewayOk: Boolean, dnsOk: Boolean, intlPct: Int, domPct: Int): Classification {
        val socialFail = results.filter { it.category == "social" && !it.ok }.size
        val intlOk = results.filter { it.id in listOf("cf", "cfdns", "gdns", "google", "github") }.count { it.ok }
        return when {
            results.all { it.ok } -> Classification("سالم", 95, "همه مقصدها با چند روش جواب دادند.")
            !gatewayOk -> Classification("احتمالی: مشکل Gateway / وای‌فای محلی", 60, "پینگ گیت‌وی مودم ناموفق است؛ مشکل احتمالاً بین گوشی و مودم است، نه اینترنت.")
            !dnsOk && results.any { it.category == "dns" && it.ok } -> Classification("احتمالی: اختلال DNS", 70, "DNS معمولی خراب ولی DoH سالم است — پاسخ DNS با DoH متفاوت است.")
            filtering.contains("TLS") -> Classification("احتمالی: فیلتر SNI/TLS", 75, "TCP وصل می‌شود ولی TLS Handshake شکست می‌خورد.")
            domPct >= 80 && intlPct < 40 -> Classification("احتمالی: اختلال مسیر بین‌المللی / ISP", 85, "داخلی سالم (≥80٪) ولی بین‌الملل قطع (<40٪) — الگوی مشاهده‌شده با محدودیت مسیر سازگار است؛ علت قطعی (ISP یا مسیر بین‌الملل) از این داده به‌تنهایی مشخص نیست.")
            socialFail >= 2 && intlOk >= 4 -> Classification("احتمالی: فیلترینگ مقصد خاص", 80, "شبکه‌های اجتماعی خراب‌اند ولی اینترنت عمومی سالم است — مشکل از سرور/فیلتر مقصد است، نه کل اینترنت.")
            results.count { it.ok } == 0 -> Classification("Unknown — قطعی کامل یا بدون اتصال", 50, "هیچ مقصدی جواب نداد؛ نمی‌توان گفت مشکل از ISP است یا دستگاه — وای‌فای/داده را چک کن.")
            else -> Classification("Unknown — شواهد کافی نیست", 50, "الگو با هیچ دسته‌بندی قطعی نمی‌خواند؛ نتیجه «احتمالی» اعلام نمی‌شود.")
        }
    }

    // Network Change Detection: compare current network fingerprint with last stored one.
    fun detectChange(ctx: Context): String {
        val conn = Geo.connectionInfo(ctx)
        val geo = Geo.fetch()
        val net = NetworkInfo.summary(ctx)
        val fingerprint = "$conn | ${geo.ip} | $net"
        val old = History.lastFingerprint(ctx)
        val now = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis())
        return if (old.isBlank()) {
            History.setLastFingerprint(ctx, fingerprint)
            "اولین ثبت شبکه در $now — $conn"
        } else if (old == fingerprint) {
            "بدون تغییر شبکه از آخرین بررسی."
        } else {
            History.setLastFingerprint(ctx, fingerprint)
            val line = "تغییر شبکه در $now: $conn • IP ${geo.ip}"
            History.addLog(ctx, line)
            History.addTimeline(ctx, "network-change", line)
            line
        }
    }

    // Stability Timeline: events recorded with exact time.
    fun perfSnapshot(ctx: Context): String {
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val usedMb = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024
        val bm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.BATTERY_SERVICE) as BatteryManager
        val level = try { bm.getIntProperty(BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_CAPACITY) } catch (e: Exception) { -1 }
        return "RAM اپ: ${usedMb}MB (Heap) • باتری: ${if (level >= 0) "$level٪" else "نامشخص"} • CPU: اندازه‌گیری دقیق CPU بدون Profiler ممکن نیست — فقط زمان هر بررسی ثبت می‌شود."
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/RestoreStream.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import android.util.JsonReader
import android.util.JsonToken
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.FilterInputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

// v2.9.4 — Restore Security (P0-2): bounded reading + streaming JSON parser.
// The old restore read the whole backup into a String and parsed it with
// JSONObject (2-4x the file size in transient heap). For a real ~29 MB backup
// that peaked at an estimated 200-300 MB — unsafe on a 256 MB heap. This file
// streams the backup through android.util.JsonReader straight into the staging
// DB in 5,000-row batches, so heap stays flat regardless of file size.
//
// Strictness is DELIBERATELY identical to the old validateRestoreJson
// (53 checks, zero getters): missing field = default, present field with the
// wrong type = reject with the field name. Two documented tightenings:
// - duplicate field names inside one object are REJECTED (JSONObject kept
//   the last one silently);
// - integer fields must be written as plain integer literals ("1" ok,
//   "1.0" / "1e3" / "01" / "+1" / "\"1\"" rejected) because JsonToken.NUMBER
//   alone does not prove the literal is an integer.
object RestoreStream {

    class SizeLimitExceeded : IOException("حجم فایل بیشتر از حد مجاز است")
    class ValidationFailed(field: String) : Exception("فیلد $field نوع معتبر ندارد")
    class CorruptBackup(msg: String) : Exception(msg)

    data class RestoreCounts(
        val samples: Int, val pingPoints: Int, val lossPoints: Int,
        val gamingSessions: Int, val gamingSamples: Int
    )

    // Prefs values carried by the backup (null = field absent -> keep current value,
    // EXCEPT inside a present "settings"/"last_check" object, where the old code
    // wrote the documented defaults for missing fields).
    data class RestorePayload(
        val log: String?, val timeline: String?, val hourly: String?, val blackbox: String?,
        val gamingLog: String?, val gamingMatches: String?, val spikes: String?,
        val intervalSec: Int?, val alertThreshold: Int?, val livePing: Boolean?,
        val customTarget: String?, val dnsRounds: Int?, val alertsOn: Boolean?,
        val lastCheck: String?, // pipe-joined, same format as History.saveLastCheck
        val counts: RestoreCounts
    )

    enum class RecoveryAction { NOTHING, CLEAN_STAGING, ROLLBACK, ROLL_FORWARD }

    // ---- Test-only hooks (null/false in production; set ONLY by Lab tests,
    // cleared in finally — same pattern as the v2.9.1 gate hooks) ----
    /** Park the parse after this many inserted rows (kill-test arming). */
    @Volatile var testStopAfterRows: Int? = null
    /** Set to true while a parse is parked by testStopAfterRows. */
    val testParked = AtomicBoolean(false)

    // ---- Pure helpers (JVM-testable) ----

    private val INT_LITERAL = Regex("^-?(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$")

    /** "1" -> true; "1.0", "1e3", "01", "+1", "\"1\"", "" -> false. */
    fun isIntegerLiteral(lit: String): Boolean = INT_LITERAL.matches(lit)

    /** True only when the size is KNOWN and over the limit (null = unknown -> rely on the during-read bound). */
    fun exceedsCap(knownSize: Long?, limitBytes: Long): Boolean =
        knownSize != null && knownSize > limitBytes

    /** Reads the whole stream but aborts as soon as the cap is exceeded. Never holds more than the cap. */
    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun readBounded(input: InputStream, limitBytes: Long): ByteArray {
        require(limitBytes >= 0) { "limit" }
        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val buf = ByteArray(65536)
        var total = 0L
        while (true) {
            val n = input.read(buf)
            if (n < 0) break
            total += n
            if (total > limitBytes) throw SizeLimitExceeded()
            out.write(buf, 0, n)
        }
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    /**
     * Pure encoding of the crash table (journal state + staging presence).
     * live/old existence does not change the ACTION — it only changes which
     * file steps the ROLLBACK/ROLL_FORWARD implementation takes.
     */
    fun decideRecovery(journalState: String?, stagingExists: Boolean): RecoveryAction {
        return when (journalState) {
            "STARTED" -> RecoveryAction.ROLLBACK
            "COMMITTED" -> RecoveryAction.ROLL_FORWARD
            else -> if (stagingExists) RecoveryAction.CLEAN_STAGING else RecoveryAction.NOTHING
        }
    }

    // ---- Streaming parser ----

    /** InputStream that throws SizeLimitExceeded as soon as more than limitBytes are read. */
    class BoundedInputStream(input: InputStream, private val limitBytes: Long) : FilterInputStream(input) {
        private var total = 0L
        override fun read(): Int {
            val b = super.read()
            if (b >= 0) {
                total++
                if (total > limitBytes) throw SizeLimitExceeded()
            }
            return b
        }
        override fun read(b: ByteArray, off: Int, len: Int): Int {
            val n = super.read(b, off, len)
            if (n > 0) {
                total += n
                if (total > limitBytes) throw SizeLimitExceeded()
            }
            return n
        }
    }

    /**
     * Streams a backup into the staging DB (batched transactions, 5,000 rows)
     * and verifies it (PRAGMA integrity_check + per-table row counts).
     * Returns the prefs payload for Phase B. Throws SizeLimitExceeded,
     * ValidationFailed or CorruptBackup; the staging DB is disposable on
     * failure (the caller deletes it). onProgress(rows) is called every
     * 1,000 inserted rows (RAM sampling by the caller).
     */
    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun streamIntoStaging(
        input: InputStream,
        limitBytes: Long,
        stagingDb: SQLiteDatabase,
        onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?
    ): RestorePayload {
        val reader = JsonReader(InputStreamReader(BoundedInputStream(input, limitBytes), Charsets.UTF_8))
        reader.isLenient = false
        val batcher = Batcher()
        val counts = MutableCounts()
        var version: Int? = null
        var samplesSeen = false
        var pingSeen = false
        var log: String? = null; var timeline: String? = null; var hourly: String? = null
        var blackbox: String? = null; var gamingLog: String? = null
        var gamingMatches: String? = null; var spikes: String? = null
        var intervalSec: Int? = null; var alertThreshold: Int? = null; var livePing: Boolean? = null
        var customTarget: String? = null; var dnsRounds: Int? = null; var alertsOn: Boolean? = null
        var lastCheck: String? = null
        try {
            if (reader.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_OBJECT) throw CorruptBackup("فایل بکاپ خراب است: ساختار JSON معتبر نیست")
            reader.beginObject()
            val seenTop = HashSet<String>()
            while (reader.hasNext()) {
                val name = reader.nextName()
                if (!seenTop.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("$name (تکراری)")
                when (name) {
                    "version" -> version = readInt(reader, "version")
                    "exported_at" -> reader.skipValue() // informational only; any type is fine
                    "samples" -> { samplesSeen = true; parseSamples(reader, stagingDb, batcher, counts, onProgress) }
                    "ping_points" -> { pingSeen = true; parsePingPoints(reader, stagingDb, batcher, counts, onProgress) }
                    "loss_points" -> parseLossPoints(reader, stagingDb, batcher, counts, onProgress)
                    "log" -> log = readString(reader, "log")
                    "timeline" -> timeline = readString(reader, "timeline")
                    "hourly" -> hourly = readString(reader, "hourly")
                    "blackbox" -> blackbox = readString(reader, "blackbox")
                    "gaming_log" -> gamingLog = readString(reader, "gaming_log")
                    "gaming_matches" -> gamingMatches = readString(reader, "gaming_matches")
                    "spikes" -> spikes = readString(reader, "spikes")
                    "gaming_sessions" -> parseGamingSessions(reader, stagingDb, batcher, counts, onProgress)
                    "settings" -> {
                        val s = parseSettings(reader)
                        intervalSec = s.intervalSec; alertThreshold = s.alertThreshold
                        livePing = s.livePing; customTarget = s.customTarget
                        dnsRounds = s.dnsRounds; alertsOn = s.alertsOn
                    }
                    "last_check" -> lastCheck = parseLastCheck(reader)
                    else -> reader.skipValue() // unknown top-level field: same as before (forward-compatible)
                }
            }
            reader.endObject()
            if (reader.peek() != JsonToken.END_DOCUMENT) throw CorruptBackup("فایل بکاپ خراب است: داده اضافی بعد از پایان فایل")
            // Pre-checks (same messages as the old restoreFromJson):
            val v = version ?: throw CorruptBackup("فایل بکاپ خراب است: نسخه فایل مشخص نیست")
            if (v != 1) throw CorruptBackup("نسخه فایل بکاپ پشتیبانی نمی‌شود: $v")
            if (!samplesSeen || !pingSeen) throw CorruptBackup("فایل بکاپ ناقص است: بخش نمونه‌ها وجود ندارد")
            batcher.finish(stagingDb)
            verifyStaging(stagingDb, counts)
        } catch (e: SizeLimitExceeded) {
            batcher.abort(stagingDb)
            throw e
        } catch (e: ValidationFailed) {
            batcher.abort(stagingDb)
            throw e
        } catch (e: CorruptBackup) {
            batcher.abort(stagingDb)
            throw e
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            batcher.abort(stagingDb)
            throw CorruptBackup("فایل بکاپ خراب است: ${e.message}")
        } finally {
            try { reader.close() } catch (_: Exception) { }
        }
        return RestorePayload(
            log, timeline, hourly, blackbox, gamingLog, gamingMatches, spikes,
            intervalSec, alertThreshold, livePing, customTarget, dnsRounds, alertsOn,
            lastCheck, RestoreCounts(counts.samples, counts.pingPoints, counts.lossPoints, counts.gamingSessions, counts.gamingSamples)
        )
    }

    private class MutableCounts {
        var samples = 0; var pingPoints = 0; var lossPoints = 0
        var gamingSessions = 0; var gamingSamples = 0
        fun total(): Int = samples + pingPoints + lossPoints + gamingSessions + gamingSamples
    }

    /** Batched transactions over the staging DB (5,000 rows per commit). */
    private class Batcher {
        private var inTx = false
        private var rows = 0
        fun insert(db: SQLiteDatabase, table: String, cv: ContentValues) {
            if (!inTx) { db.beginTransaction(); inTx = true }
            db.insert(table, null, cv)
            rows++
            if (rows >= 5000) {
                db.setTransactionSuccessful(); db.endTransaction()
                inTx = false; rows = 0
            }
        }
        fun finish(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
            if (inTx) { db.setTransactionSuccessful(); db.endTransaction(); inTx = false; rows = 0 }
        }
        fun abort(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
            if (inTx) { try { db.endTransaction() } catch (_: Exception) { }; inTx = false; rows = 0 }
        }
    }

    private fun verifyStaging(db: SQLiteDatabase, counts: MutableCounts) {
        db.rawQuery("PRAGMA integrity_check", null).use { c ->
            if (!c.moveToFirst() || c.getString(0) != "ok") throw CorruptBackup("پایگاه موقت سالم نیست")
        }
        val actual = mapOf(
            "samples" to counts.samples, "ping_points" to counts.pingPoints,
            "loss_points" to counts.lossPoints, "gaming_sessions" to counts.gamingSessions,
            "gaming_samples" to counts.gamingSamples
        )
        for ((table, expected) in actual) {
            db.rawQuery("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table", null).use { c ->
                c.moveToFirst()
                if (c.getLong(0) != expected.toLong()) throw CorruptBackup("پایگاه موقت ناقص است: $table")
            }
        }
    }

    /** Per-row tick: park hook + progress callback (every 1,000 rows). */
    private fun tick(counts: MutableCounts, onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?) {
        val t = counts.total()
        maybePark(t)
        if (onProgress != null && t % 1000 == 0) onProgress(t)
    }

    /** Test hook: parks the parse (sets testParked) until testStopAfterRows is cleared. */
    private fun maybePark(totalRows: Int) {
        val stop = testStopAfterRows ?: return
        if (totalRows < stop) return
        testParked.set(true)
        while (testStopAfterRows != null) {
            try { Thread.sleep(50) } catch (_: InterruptedException) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); break }
        }
        testParked.set(false)
    }

    // ---- Typed readers (peek BEFORE reading; JsonReader.nextInt() is lenient and must not be used) ----

    private fun readInt(r: JsonReader, field: String): Int {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.NUMBER) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        val lit = r.nextString()
        if (!isIntegerLiteral(lit)) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        val v = lit.toLongOrNull() ?: throw ValidationFailed(field)
        if (v < Int.MIN_VALUE || v > Int.MAX_VALUE) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        return v.toInt()
    }

    private fun readLong(r: JsonReader, field: String): Long {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.NUMBER) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        val lit = r.nextString()
        if (!isIntegerLiteral(lit)) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        return lit.toLongOrNull() ?: throw ValidationFailed(field)
    }

    private fun readDouble(r: JsonReader, field: String): Double {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.NUMBER) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        val lit = r.nextString()
        val v = lit.toDoubleOrNull() ?: throw ValidationFailed(field)
        if (!v.isFinite()) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        return v
    }

    private fun readString(r: JsonReader, field: String): String {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.STRING) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        return r.nextString()
    }

    private fun readBool(r: JsonReader, field: String): Boolean {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BOOLEAN) throw ValidationFailed(field)
        return r.nextBoolean()
    }

    private fun readIntOrNull(r: JsonReader, field: String): Int? {
        if (r.peek() == JsonToken.NULL) { r.nextNull(); return null }
        return readInt(r, field)
    }

    // ---- Row parsers (required fields reject with the field name; optional fields keep the old defaults) ----

    private fun parseSamples(r: JsonReader, db: SQLiteDatabase, b: Batcher, counts: MutableCounts, onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?) {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY) throw ValidationFailed("samples")
        r.beginArray()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            r.beginObject()
            var time: Long? = null; var ok: Int? = null; var total: Int? = null
            var avg = -1.0; var jit = -1.0; var loss = -1.0
            val seen = HashSet<String>()
            while (r.hasNext()) {
                val name = r.nextName()
                if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("samples.$name (تکراری)")
                when (name) {
                    "time" -> time = readLong(r, "samples.time")
                    "ok" -> ok = readInt(r, "samples.ok")
                    "total" -> total = readInt(r, "samples.total")
                    "avg_ms" -> avg = readDouble(r, "samples.avg_ms")
                    "jitter_ms" -> jit = readDouble(r, "samples.jitter_ms")
                    "loss_pct" -> loss = readDouble(r, "samples.loss_pct")
                    else -> r.skipValue()
                }
            }
            r.endObject()
            val t = time ?: throw ValidationFailed("samples.time (وجود ندارد)")
            val o = ok ?: throw ValidationFailed("samples.ok (وجود ندارد)")
            val tot = total ?: throw ValidationFailed("samples.total (وجود ندارد)")
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put("time", t); put("ok", o); put("total", tot)
                put("avg_ms", avg); put("jitter_ms", jit); put("loss_pct", loss)
                put("source", "restore")
            }
            b.insert(db, "samples", cv)
            counts.samples++
            tick(counts, onProgress)
        }
        r.endArray()
    }

    private fun parsePingPoints(r: JsonReader, db: SQLiteDatabase, b: Batcher, counts: MutableCounts, onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?) {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY) throw ValidationFailed("ping_points")
        r.beginArray()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            r.beginObject()
            var time: Long? = null; var ms: Double? = null
            val seen = HashSet<String>()
            while (r.hasNext()) {
                val name = r.nextName()
                if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("ping_points.$name (تکراری)")
                when (name) {
                    "time" -> time = readLong(r, "ping_points.time")
                    "ms" -> ms = readDouble(r, "ping_points.ms")
                    else -> r.skipValue()
                }
            }
            r.endObject()
            val t = time ?: throw ValidationFailed("ping_points.time (وجود ندارد)")
            val m = ms ?: throw ValidationFailed("ping_points.ms (وجود ندارد)")
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put("time", t); put("ms", m); put("ok", if (m >= 0) 1 else 0)
            }
            b.insert(db, "ping_points", cv)
            counts.pingPoints++
            tick(counts, onProgress)
        }
        r.endArray()
    }

    private fun parseLossPoints(r: JsonReader, db: SQLiteDatabase, b: Batcher, counts: MutableCounts, onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?) {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY) throw ValidationFailed("loss_points")
        r.beginArray()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            r.beginObject()
            var time: Long? = null; var loss: Double? = null
            val seen = HashSet<String>()
            while (r.hasNext()) {
                val name = r.nextName()
                if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("loss_points.$name (تکراری)")
                when (name) {
                    "time" -> time = readLong(r, "loss_points.time")
                    "loss" -> loss = readDouble(r, "loss_points.loss")
                    else -> r.skipValue()
                }
            }
            r.endObject()
            val t = time ?: throw ValidationFailed("loss_points.time (وجود ندارد)")
            val l = loss ?: throw ValidationFailed("loss_points.loss (وجود ندارد)")
            val cv = ContentValues().apply { put("time", t); put("loss", l) }
            b.insert(db, "loss_points", cv)
            counts.lossPoints++
            tick(counts, onProgress)
        }
        r.endArray()
    }

    private fun parseGamingSessions(r: JsonReader, db: SQLiteDatabase, b: Batcher, counts: MutableCounts, onProgress: ((Int) -> Unit)?) {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY) throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions")
        r.beginArray()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            r.beginObject()
            var started: Long? = null; var ended: Long? = null
            var profile = ""; var region = ""; var host = ""
            var sent: Int? = null; var received: Int? = null
            var loss: Double? = null; var avg: Double? = null; var min: Double? = null
            var max: Double? = null; var jit: Double? = null; var p95: Double? = null
            var spikes: Int? = null; var burst: Int? = null
            var score: Int? = null; var scoreSeen = false
            var bbGrade = ""
            val nested = ArrayList<Pair<Long, Double>>()
            val seen = HashSet<String>()
            while (r.hasNext()) {
                val name = r.nextName()
                if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.$name (تکراری)")
                when (name) {
                    "started" -> started = readLong(r, "gaming_sessions.started")
                    "ended" -> ended = readLong(r, "gaming_sessions.ended")
                    "profile" -> profile = readString(r, "gaming_sessions.profile")
                    "region" -> region = readString(r, "gaming_sessions.region")
                    "host" -> host = readString(r, "gaming_sessions.host")
                    "sent" -> sent = readInt(r, "gaming_sessions.sent")
                    "received" -> received = readInt(r, "gaming_sessions.received")
                    "loss" -> loss = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.loss")
                    "avg_ms" -> avg = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.avg_ms")
                    "min_ms" -> min = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.min_ms")
                    "max_ms" -> max = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.max_ms")
                    "jitter_ms" -> jit = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.jitter_ms")
                    "p95" -> p95 = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.p95")
                    "spikes" -> spikes = readInt(r, "gaming_sessions.spikes")
                    "burst" -> burst = readInt(r, "gaming_sessions.burst")
                    "score" -> { score = readIntOrNull(r, "gaming_sessions.score"); scoreSeen = true }
                    "bb_grade" -> bbGrade = readString(r, "gaming_sessions.bb_grade")
                    "samples" -> {
                        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY) throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.samples")
                        r.beginArray()
                        while (r.hasNext()) {
                            r.beginObject()
                            var st: Long? = null; var sm: Double? = null
                            val sSeen = HashSet<String>()
                            while (r.hasNext()) {
                                val sn = r.nextName()
                                if (!sSeen.add(sn)) throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.samples.$sn (تکراری)")
                                when (sn) {
                                    "time" -> st = readLong(r, "gaming_sessions.samples.time")
                                    "ms" -> sm = readDouble(r, "gaming_sessions.samples.ms")
                                    else -> r.skipValue()
                                }
                            }
                            r.endObject()
                            val stt = st ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.samples.time (وجود ندارد)")
                            val smm = sm ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.samples.ms (وجود ندارد)")
                            nested.add(Pair(stt, smm))
                        }
                        r.endArray()
                    }
                    else -> r.skipValue()
                }
            }
            r.endObject()
            val st = started ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.started (وجود ندارد)")
            val en = ended ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.ended (وجود ندارد)")
            val se = sent ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.sent (وجود ندارد)")
            val re = received ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.received (وجود ندارد)")
            val lo = loss ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.loss (وجود ندارد)")
            val av = avg ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.avg_ms (وجود ندارد)")
            val mi = min ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.min_ms (وجود ندارد)")
            val ma = max ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.max_ms (وجود ندارد)")
            val ji = jit ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.jitter_ms (وجود ندارد)")
            val p9 = p95 ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.p95 (وجود ندارد)")
            val sp = spikes ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.spikes (وجود ندارد)")
            val bu = burst ?: throw ValidationFailed("gaming_sessions.burst (وجود ندارد)")
            val sc = if (scoreSeen) score else null
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put("started", st); put("ended", en); put("profile", profile); put("region", region)
                put("host", host); put("sent", se); put("received", re); put("loss", lo)
                put("avg_ms", av); put("min_ms", mi); put("max_ms", ma); put("jitter_ms", ji)
                put("p95", p9); put("spikes", sp); put("burst", bu)
                if (sc == null) putNull("score") else put("score", sc)
                put("bb_grade", bbGrade)
            }
            // NOTE: the session row is inserted directly (not through the Batcher)
            // because the returned row id is needed for its samples. It lands in
            // the Batcher's open transaction when one is active, or auto-commits
            // otherwise; staging is disposable on failure either way.
            val sessionId = db.insert("gaming_sessions", null, cv)
            counts.gamingSessions++
            tick(counts, onProgress)
            for ((stt, smm) in nested) {
                val scv = ContentValues().apply {
                    put("session_id", sessionId); put("time", stt); put("ms", smm)
                    put("ok", if (smm >= 0) 1 else 0)
                }
                b.insert(db, "gaming_samples", scv)
                counts.gamingSamples++
                tick(counts, onProgress)
            }
        }
        r.endArray()
    }

    private data class SettingsParsed(
        val intervalSec: Int, val alertThreshold: Int, val livePing: Boolean,
        val customTarget: String, val dnsRounds: Int, val alertsOn: Boolean
    )

    private fun parseSettings(r: JsonReader): SettingsParsed {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_OBJECT) throw ValidationFailed("settings")
        var intervalSec = 30; var alertThreshold = 40; var livePing = false
        var customTarget = ""; var dnsRounds = 20; var alertsOn = true
        r.beginObject()
        val seen = HashSet<String>()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            val name = r.nextName()
            if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("settings.$name (تکراری)")
            when (name) {
                "interval_sec" -> intervalSec = readInt(r, "settings.interval_sec")
                "alert_threshold" -> alertThreshold = readInt(r, "settings.alert_threshold")
                "live_ping" -> livePing = readBool(r, "settings.live_ping")
                "custom_target" -> customTarget = readString(r, "settings.custom_target")
                "dns_rounds" -> dnsRounds = readInt(r, "settings.dns_rounds")
                "alerts_on" -> alertsOn = readBool(r, "settings.alerts_on")
                else -> r.skipValue()
            }
        }
        r.endObject()
        return SettingsParsed(intervalSec, alertThreshold, livePing, customTarget, dnsRounds, alertsOn)
    }

    private fun parseLastCheck(r: JsonReader): String {
        if (r.peek() != JsonToken.BEGIN_OBJECT) throw ValidationFailed("last_check")
        var ping = -1.0; var jit = -1.0; var loss = -1.0
        var intl = 0; var dom = 0
        var score: Int? = null; var scoreSeen = false
        var status = ""; var checkedAt = 0L
        r.beginObject()
        val seen = HashSet<String>()
        while (r.hasNext()) {
            val name = r.nextName()
            if (!seen.add(name)) throw ValidationFailed("last_check.$name (تکراری)")
            when (name) {
                "ping_avg" -> ping = readDouble(r, "last_check.ping_avg")
                "jitter_ms" -> jit = readDouble(r, "last_check.jitter_ms")
                "loss_pct" -> loss = readDouble(r, "last_check.loss_pct")
                "intl_pct" -> intl = readInt(r, "last_check.intl_pct")
                "dom_pct" -> dom = readInt(r, "last_check.dom_pct")
                "score_total" -> { score = readIntOrNull(r, "last_check.score_total"); scoreSeen = true }
                "score_status" -> status = readString(r, "last_check.score_status")
                "checked_at" -> checkedAt = readLong(r, "last_check.checked_at")
                else -> r.skipValue()
            }
        }
        r.endObject()
        // Same pipe format as History.saveLastCheck.
        val sc = if (scoreSeen) score else null
        return listOf(
            ping.toString(), jit.toString(), loss.toString(),
            intl.toString(), dom.toString(),
            if (sc == null) "" else sc.toString(),
            status, checkedAt.toString()
        ).joinToString("|")
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/SparklineView.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.View

// Mini sparkline for the Home stat cards (v2.6).
// - Invalid values (NaN, negative) are filtered out before drawing — they
//   used to corrupt the scale.
// - Points spread across the full width (the old code divided by a fixed 59,
//   so fewer points were squeezed into the left corner).
// - Flat data (all values equal) draws a flat line instead of a broken scale.
class SparklineView @JvmOverloads constructor(ctx: Context, attrs: AttributeSet? = null) : View(ctx, attrs) {
    var values: List<Double> = emptyList()
        set(v) { field = v.filter { !it.isNaN() && it >= 0 }; invalidate() }
    var lineColor: Int = Color.parseColor("#2DD4BF")
    var fillColor: Int = Color.parseColor("#142E2B")

    private val linePaint = Paint().apply { strokeWidth = 4f; isAntiAlias = true; style = Paint.Style.STROKE }
    private val fillPaint = Paint().apply { style = Paint.Style.FILL; alpha = 140 }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        if (values.size < 2) return
        val w = width.toFloat(); val h = height.toFloat()
        val maxV = values.maxOrNull() ?: 0.0
        val minV = values.minOrNull() ?: 0.0
        val span = (maxV - minV).coerceAtLeast(1.0)
        linePaint.color = lineColor; fillPaint.color = fillColor
        val path = Path(); val fill = Path()
        val n = values.size
        val stepX = if (n > 1) w / (n - 1) else w
        for (i in values.indices) {
            val x = i * stepX
            val y = h - 4f - ((values[i] - minV) / span * (h - 8f)).toFloat()
            if (i == 0) { path.moveTo(x, y); fill.moveTo(x, h); fill.lineTo(x, y) } else { path.lineTo(x, y); fill.lineTo(x, y) }
        }
        fill.lineTo((n - 1) * stepX, h); fill.close()
        canvas.drawPath(fill, fillPaint)
        canvas.drawPath(path, linePaint)
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/aksell/netwatch/WidgetStats.kt
================================================================================
package com.aksell.netwatch

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.net.TrafficStats
import android.os.Build

// Live Network Statistics for the home-screen widget (v2.9.6, Scope B).
// Everything here is read from real sources; nothing is estimated or
// invented:
// - Connection type: NetworkCapabilities TRANSPORT only (Wi-Fi / Mobile
//   Data / VPN / Ethernet). No TelephonyManager, no READ_PHONE_STATE.
// - Speeds: TrafficStats deltas across the widget's EXISTING refresh
//   cadence (the previous rx/tx + timestamp live in prefs "widget_net").
//   The first run has no previous sample and shows "—", never a fake 0,
//   and the value is always labelled with its measured window.
// - Data usage: TrafficStats device totals since boot, labelled as such.
// - DNS / gateway / public IP: cached (value + timestamp) and refreshed
//   ONLY by MonitorService's cycle via refreshNetworkStats() — never by
//   the widget itself, so the widget never does network I/O.
// - When offline, whatever is still extractable (local IP, connection
//   type, data usage, speeds) is still shown; unknown values are "—".
object WidgetStats {
    private const val PREFS = "widget_net"
    private const val PUBLIC_IP_MAX_AGE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000L

    // ---- Pure formatters (JVM-tested in selfTests()) ----

    // Bytes/sec -> "12.0 Mbps" / "800 Kbps" / "800 bps". Negative or NaN
    // (no measurement) is "—"; a measured 0 is honestly "0 bps".
    fun formatSpeed(bytesPerSec: Double): String {
        if (bytesPerSec.isNaN() || bytesPerSec < 0) return "—"
        val bps = bytesPerSec * 8.0
        return when {
            bps >= 1_000_000 -> "${"%.1f".format(bps / 1_000_000)} Mbps"
            bps >= 1_000 -> "${"%.0f".format(bps / 1_000)} Kbps"
            else -> "${"%.0f".format(bps)} bps"
        }
    }

    // Device total bytes -> "3.2 GB" / "2.5 MB" / "1.5 KB" / "0 B".
    // Negative (TrafficStats unsupported) is "—".
    fun formatBytes(totalBytes: Long): String {
        if (totalBytes < 0) return "—"
        return when {
            totalBytes >= 1_000_000_000L -> "${"%.1f".format(totalBytes / 1e9)} GB"
            totalBytes >= 1_000_000L -> "${"%.1f".format(totalBytes / 1e6)} MB"
            totalBytes >= 1_000L -> "${"%.1f".format(totalBytes / 1e3)} KB"
            else -> "$totalBytes B"
        }
    }

    // Milliseconds -> "42 ms"; a failed/unknown measurement (< 0) is "—".
    fun formatMs(ms: Long): String = if (ms < 0) "—" else "$ms ms"

    // Persian relative age ("۵ دقیقه پیش", "همین حالا"). Pure.
    fun ageFa(ts: Long, nowMs: Long): String {
        if (ts <= 0) return "نامشخص"
        val m = (nowMs - ts) / 60000
        val fa = { n: Long -> n.toString().map { c -> "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹".getOrNull(c - '0') ?: c }.joinToString("") }
        return when {
            m < 1 -> "همین حالا"
            m < 60 -> "${fa(m)} دقیقه پیش"
            m < 1440 -> "${fa(m / 60)} ساعت پیش"
            else -> "${fa(m / 1440)} روز پیش"
        }
    }

    // Bufferbloat tile display (grade, sub, colour), pure. Four distinct
    // states, never a fake value:
    // - Testing wins over any stored result (a re-test is in progress).
    // - null stored result -> Not Tested ("اندازه‌گیری نشده", "—", gray).
    // - valid=false -> Failed ("ناموفق", "—", red; the reason lives in
    //   GamingActivity, which shows bb.note in full).
    // - valid -> grade A–F coloured + "idle→loaded ms • age".
    fun bufferbloatDisplay(bb: Gaming.Bufferbloat?, measuredAtMs: Long, testing: Boolean, nowMs: Long): Triple<String, String, String> {
        if (testing) return Triple("…", "در حال اندازه‌گیری", "#F59E0B")
        if (bb == null) return Triple("—", "اندازه‌گیری نشده", "#94A3B8")
        if (!bb.valid) return Triple("—", "ناموفق", "#EF4444")
        val color = when (bb.grade) {
            "A" -> "#22C55E"
            "B" -> "#4ADE80"
            "C" -> "#F59E0B"
            "D" -> "#F97316"
            else -> "#EF4444"
        }
        val sub = "${"%.0f".format(bb.idleMs)}→${"%.0f".format(bb.loadedMs)}ms • ${ageFa(measuredAtMs, nowMs)}"
        return Triple(bb.grade, sub, color)
    }

    // ---- Connection type: transports only ----

    fun transportType(ctx: Context): String {
        return try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23) return "—"
            val cm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
            val net = cm.activeNetwork ?: return "—"
            val caps = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(net) ?: return "—"
            when {
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_VPN) -> "VPN"
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) -> "Wi-Fi"
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) -> "Mobile Data"
                caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_ETHERNET) -> "Ethernet"
                else -> "—"
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
    }

    // ---- Cached network stats (refreshed by MonitorService only) ----

    private fun prefs(ctx: Context) = ctx.getSharedPreferences(PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private fun fetchPublicIp(): String? {
        var conn: java.net.HttpURLConnection? = null
        return try {
            conn = java.net.URL("https://api.ipify.org?format=json").openConnection() as java.net.HttpURLConnection
            conn.connectTimeout = 5000; conn.readTimeout = 5000
            val body = conn.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText()
            org.json.JSONObject(body).optString("ip", "").takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        } catch (e: Exception) { null } finally { try { conn?.disconnect() } catch (e: Exception) {} }
    }

    // Called ONLY from MonitorService's existing cycle (background thread).
    // Bounded: DNS ~4s socket timeout, gateway ping is one bounded burst,
    // public IP only when the cached value is older than 10 min. Never
    // throws and never breaks the cycle: every item is guarded on its own.
    fun refreshNetworkStats(ctx: Context) {
        try {
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val p = prefs(ctx)
            try {
                val t = NetworkEngine.dns("google.com")
                p.edit().putLong("dns_ms", if (t.ok) t.ms else -1L).putLong("dns_ts", now).apply()
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
            try {
                val gw = NetworkInfo.gateway(ctx)
                if (gw != "—" && gw.isNotBlank()) {
                    val s = NetworkEngine.pingStats(gw)
                    p.edit().putLong("gw_ms", if (s.ok) s.avgMs.toLong() else -1L).putLong("gw_ts", now).apply()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
            try {
                val lastPub = p.getLong("pub_ts", 0L)
                if (now - lastPub > PUBLIC_IP_MAX_AGE_MS) {
                    val ip = fetchPublicIp()
                    if (ip != null) p.edit().putString("pub_ip", ip).putLong("pub_ts", now).apply()
                    // A failed fetch leaves pub_ts untouched: next cycle
                    // retries instead of stamping a failure as fresh.
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
            try { p.edit().putLong("net_ts", now).apply() } catch (e: Exception) {}
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    // ---- Snapshot for the widget (no network I/O here) ----

    data class NetSnapshot(
        val down: String, val up: String,
        val dns: String, val gateway: String,
        val localIp: String, val publicIp: String,
        val connType: String, val usage: String,
        val dnsOk: Boolean?, val gwOk: Boolean?,
        val speedWindowSec: Long, val updatedAtMs: Long
    )

    fun snapshot(ctx: Context): NetSnapshot {
        return try {
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val p = prefs(ctx)
            // Speeds from TrafficStats deltas over the widget's own refresh
            // cadence. First run (or a counter reset, e.g. reboot) stores
            // the baseline and reports "—" — never a fake 0.
            var downText = "—"
            var upText = "—"
            var windowSec = 0L
            try {
                val rx = TrafficStats.getTotalRxBytes()
                val tx = TrafficStats.getTotalTxBytes()
                if (rx >= 0 && tx >= 0) {
                    val prevRx = p.getLong("prev_rx", -1L)
                    val prevTx = p.getLong("prev_tx", -1L)
                    val prevTs = p.getLong("prev_ts", 0L)
                    val dtSec = (now - prevTs) / 1000.0
                    if (prevRx >= 0 && prevTx >= 0 && prevTs > 0 && dtSec >= 1.0) {
                        val dRx = rx - prevRx
                        val dTx = tx - prevTx
                        if (dRx >= 0 && dTx >= 0) {
                            windowSec = (now - prevTs) / 1000
                            downText = "${formatSpeed(dRx / dtSec)} (${windowSec}s)"
                            upText = "${formatSpeed(dTx / dtSec)} (${windowSec}s)"
                        }
                    }
                    p.edit().putLong("prev_rx", rx).putLong("prev_tx", tx).putLong("prev_ts", now).apply()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {}
            // Cached DNS / gateway: ms < 0 means the last attempt failed
            // ("ناموفق"), absent means never measured ("—").
            val dnsMs = if (p.contains("dns_ms")) p.getLong("dns_ms", -1L) else Long.MIN_VALUE
            val gwMs = if (p.contains("gw_ms")) p.getLong("gw_ms", -1L) else Long.MIN_VALUE
            val dnsText = when {
                dnsMs == Long.MIN_VALUE -> "—"
                dnsMs < 0 -> "ناموفق"
                else -> formatMs(dnsMs)
            }
            val gwText = when {
                gwMs == Long.MIN_VALUE -> "—"
                gwMs < 0 -> "ناموفق"
                else -> formatMs(gwMs)
            }
            val dnsOk: Boolean? = when {
                dnsMs == Long.MIN_VALUE -> null
                dnsMs < 0 -> false
                else -> true
            }
            val gwOk: Boolean? = when {
                gwMs == Long.MIN_VALUE -> null
                gwMs < 0 -> false
                else -> true
            }
            val localIp = try { NetworkInfo.localIp(ctx) } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
            val publicIp = p.getString("pub_ip", "") ?: ""
            val usage = try {
                val rx = TrafficStats.getTotalRxBytes()
                val tx = TrafficStats.getTotalTxBytes()
                if (rx < 0 || tx < 0) "—" else formatBytes(rx + tx)
            } catch (e: Exception) { "—" }
            val updatedAt = maxOf(p.getLong("dns_ts", 0L), p.getLong("gw_ts", 0L), p.getLong("pub_ts", 0L))
            NetSnapshot(
                downText, upText, dnsText, gwText,
                localIp, if (publicIp.isBlank()) "—" else publicIp,
                transportType(ctx), usage, dnsOk, gwOk, windowSec, updatedAt
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            NetSnapshot("—", "—", "—", "—", "—", "—", "—", "—", null, null, 0L, 0L)
        }
    }

    // ---- JVM self-tests (pure; called by the parent's harness) ----

    fun selfTests(): List<TestResult> {
        val out = mutableListOf<TestResult>()
        var lastT = System.currentTimeMillis()
        fun stamp(): Long { val n = System.currentTimeMillis(); val d = n - lastT; lastT = n; return d }
        fun t(name: String, expected: String, actual: String, ok: Boolean) {
            out.add(TestResult(name, "Unit — ورودی ثابت، بدون شبکه", expected, actual, ok, date = History.timeStr(System.currentTimeMillis()), durationMs = stamp()))
        }
        // formatSpeed: bytes/sec -> bits/sec display.
        t("WidgetStats — formatSpeed Mbps", "1,500,000 B/s → 12.0 Mbps", formatSpeed(1_500_000.0), formatSpeed(1_500_000.0) == "12.0 Mbps")
        t("WidgetStats — formatSpeed Kbps", "100,000 B/s → 800 Kbps", formatSpeed(100_000.0), formatSpeed(100_000.0) == "800 Kbps")
        t("WidgetStats — formatSpeed bps", "100 B/s → 800 bps", formatSpeed(100.0), formatSpeed(100.0) == "800 bps")
        t("WidgetStats — formatSpeed صفر", "0 B/s → 0 bps (مقدار واقعی، نه خطا)", formatSpeed(0.0), formatSpeed(0.0) == "0 bps")
        t("WidgetStats — formatSpeed نامعتبر", "منفی و NaN → —", "${formatSpeed(-1.0)} / ${formatSpeed(Double.NaN)}", formatSpeed(-1.0) == "—" && formatSpeed(Double.NaN) == "—")
        // formatBytes: device totals.
        t("WidgetStats — formatBytes B", "0 → 0 B", formatBytes(0L), formatBytes(0L) == "0 B")
        t("WidgetStats — formatBytes KB", "1536 → 1.5 KB", formatBytes(1536L), formatBytes(1536L) == "1.5 KB")
        t("WidgetStats — formatBytes MB", "2,500,000 → 2.5 MB", formatBytes(2_500_000L), formatBytes(2_500_000L) == "2.5 MB")
        t("WidgetStats — formatBytes GB", "3,221,225,472 → 3.2 GB", formatBytes(3_221_225_472L), formatBytes(3_221_225_472L) == "3.2 GB")
        t("WidgetStats — formatBytes نامعتبر", "منفی → —", formatBytes(-5L), formatBytes(-5L) == "—")
        // Bufferbloat display mapping: the four widget states.
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val notTested = bufferbloatDisplay(null, 0L, false, now)
        t("WidgetStats — Bufferbloat اندازه‌گیری‌نشده", "— • اندازه‌گیری نشده • خاکستری", "${notTested.first} • ${notTested.second} • ${notTested.third}", notTested == Triple("—", "اندازه‌گیری نشده", "#94A3B8"))
        val validBb = Gaming.Bufferbloat(48.0, 60.0, 12.0, "A", 100, true, 12.0, true, "ok")
        val testing = bufferbloatDisplay(validBb, now - 5 * 60_000L, true, now)
        t("WidgetStats — Bufferbloat در حال تست", "تست بر نتیجه قبلی غلبه می‌کند • کهربایی", "${testing.first} • ${testing.second} • ${testing.third}", testing == Triple("…", "در حال اندازه‌گیری", "#F59E0B"))
        val failedBb = Gaming.Bufferbloat(48.0, 0.0, 0.0, "-", null, valid = false, note = "پینگ زیر بار ناکافی است")
        val failed = bufferbloatDisplay(failedBb, now - 5 * 60_000L, false, now)
        t("WidgetStats — Bufferbloat ناموفق", "— • ناموفق • قرمز", "${failed.first} • ${failed.second} • ${failed.third}", failed == Triple("—", "ناموفق", "#EF4444"))
        val measured = bufferbloatDisplay(validBb, now - 5 * 60_000L, false, now)
        t("WidgetStats — Bufferbloat اندازه‌گیری‌شده", "A • ‏48→60ms • ۵ دقیقه پیش • سبز", "${measured.first} • ${measured.second} • ${measured.third}", measured.first == "A" && measured.second == "48→60ms • ۵ دقیقه پیش" && measured.third == "#22C55E")
        val measuredF = bufferbloatDisplay(Gaming.Bufferbloat(50.0, 300.0, 250.0, "F", 20, true, 12.0, true, "ok"), now - 90_000L, false, now)
        t("WidgetStats — Bufferbloat درجه F", "F • ‏50→300ms • ۱ دقیقه پیش • قرمز", "${measuredF.first} • ${measuredF.second} • ${measuredF.third}", measuredF.first == "F" && measuredF.second == "50→300ms • ۱ دقیقه پیش" && measuredF.third == "#EF4444")
        // ageFa edge cases.
        t("WidgetStats — ageFa همین حالا", "کمتر از ۱ دقیقه → همین حالا", ageFa(now - 30_000L, now), ageFa(now - 30_000L, now) == "همین حالا")
        t("WidgetStats — ageFa ساعت", "۹۰ دقیقه → ۱ ساعت پیش", ageFa(now - 90 * 60_000L, now), ageFa(now - 90 * 60_000L, now) == "۱ ساعت پیش")
        t("WidgetStats — ageFa نامشخص", "ts=0 → نامشخص", ageFa(0L, now), ageFa(0L, now) == "نامشخص")
        // Bufferbloat record round-trip (Gaming pure format/parse).
        val rec = Gaming.formatBufferbloatRecord(1_700_000_000_000L, validBb)
        val parsed = Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord(rec)
        t("WidgetStats — رکورد Bufferbloat رفت‌وبرگشت", "همه فیلدها (زمان، idle، loaded، درجه، امتیاز، valid) حفظ شوند", parsed?.let { "${it.ts}|${it.bb.idleMs}|${it.bb.loadedMs}|${it.bb.grade}|${it.bb.score}|${it.bb.valid}" } ?: "null", parsed != null && parsed.ts == 1_700_000_000_000L && parsed.bb.idleMs == 48.0 && parsed.bb.loadedMs == 60.0 && parsed.bb.grade == "A" && parsed.bb.score == 100 && parsed.bb.valid)
        val recPipeNote = Gaming.formatBufferbloatRecord(1_700_000_000_000L, Gaming.Bufferbloat(48.0, 60.0, 12.0, "A", 100, valid = true, note = "a|b"))
        val parsedPipe = Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord(recPipeNote)
        t("WidgetStats — رکورد با | در یادداشت", "یادداشت حاوی | قالب را نمی‌شکند (۸ فیلد)", parsedPipe?.bb?.grade ?: "null", parsedPipe != null && parsedPipe.bb.grade == "A")
        t("WidgetStats — رکورد خراب", "رشته خالی و ناقص → null", "${Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord("")} / ${Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord("1|2|3")}", Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord("") == null && Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord("1|2|3") == null && Gaming.parseBufferbloatRecord("not-a-number|48|60|12|A|100|true|x") == null)
        // lossBuckets (ChartData pure helper): the loss strip's data.
        val w0 = 1_700_000_000_000L
        val w1 = w0 + 60_000L
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets خالی", "بدون نقطه → همه صفر", ChartData.lossBuckets(emptyList(), w0, w1, 4).toString(), ChartData.lossBuckets(emptyList(), w0, w1, 4) == listOf(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
        val allFail = (0 until 10).map { w0 + it * 6_000L to -1.0 }
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets همه ناموفق", "۱۰ نقطه ناموفق → همه باکت‌ها 1.0", ChartData.lossBuckets(allFail, w0, w1, 6).toString(), ChartData.lossBuckets(allFail, w0, w1, 6).all { it == 1.0 })
        val mixed = listOf(w0 + 1_000L to 50.0, w0 + 2_000L to -1.0, w0 + 31_000L to 60.0, w0 + 32_000L to 70.0)
        val mixedBuckets = ChartData.lossBuckets(mixed, w0, w1, 2)
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets مختلط", "باکت اول ۱/۲ ناموفق=0.5 • باکت دوم 0/۲=0.0", mixedBuckets.toString(), mixedBuckets == listOf(0.5, 0.0))
        val outside = listOf(w0 - 10_000L to -1.0, w0 + 5_000L to 50.0, w1 + 10_000L to -1.0)
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets خارج از بازه", "نقاط بیرون بازه نادیده گرفته شوند → همه صفر", ChartData.lossBuckets(outside, w0, w1, 2).toString(), ChartData.lossBuckets(outside, w0, w1, 2) == listOf(0.0, 0.0))
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets لبه‌ها", "bucketCount=0 → خالی • بازه معکوس → صفرها", "${ChartData.lossBuckets(mixed, w0, w1, 0)} / ${ChartData.lossBuckets(mixed, w1, w0, 3)}", ChartData.lossBuckets(mixed, w0, w1, 0).isEmpty() && ChartData.lossBuckets(mixed, w1, w0, 3) == listOf(0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
        val nanPts = listOf(w0 + 1_000L to Double.NaN, w0 + 2_000L to 50.0)
        t("WidgetStats — lossBuckets NaN", "NaN مثل ناموفق شمرده شود → 0.5", ChartData.lossBuckets(nanPts, w0, w1, 1).toString(), ChartData.lossBuckets(nanPts, w0, w1, 1) == listOf(0.5))
        return out
    }
}

     The project has no androidx FileProvider (zero dependencies), so legacy
     (API 21-28) export files are served by the minimal read-only
     ExportFileProvider; this file documents the shared folder path for
     that provider and for any future file-sharing use. API 29+ shares the
     MediaStore content Uri directly. -->
<paths>
    <external-path name="netwatch_downloads" path="Download/NetWatch/" />
